# Special subspaces U: results (specialscan.py, see agents/special.md)

Generated by special_report.py from special/res/*.jsonl.  Target: rank r vs r + 1 for r in the "ranks" column; "H1 generic" = rank of one generic functional at the evaluated tensor ranks.  Line points: kind (line = random line, in = line inside a linear drop component, thru = line through a natural point), e = degree of the point's field F_{p^e}, multiplicity over all lines, [rank profile r:rank].  W(k) = linear drop component of dimension k (tested as U with H and V prefixes).

## Summary (curated; the tables below are generated)

**Question** (PROMPT_specialU.md): do *special* subspaces U of the multiplicity space separate tensor ranks that
generic U do not -- in a degree below the scan's lowest separating degree, or at ranks the scan has not separated?

**Answer: no** (all planned runs complete, 2026-10-19).  No special U separates a target rank in any case checked (hits: none).  Positive controls
reproduced first (special/logs/controls.log): C^3 d=6 ((4,2),(3,2,1),(3,2,1)) dir 1, rank 4 vs 5: drop point 26 vs
27 (generic 27/27); C^4 d=8 ((6,2),(3,2,2,1),(3,2,2,1)) dir 1, rank 6 vs 7: drop point 220 vs 225 (generic 225/225).

| format | target ranks | degrees checked with special U | methods | outcome |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 4x4x4 | 6 vs 7 (generic: lowest d = 8) | 5, 6, 7 (all g >= 2 components, all directions) | line, linear components + recursion, U_k flag / swaps / intersections (H, V, all dims), lines through / inside natural U; plane resultants (codim 2) and Grassmannian pencils for all g >= 3 | not separated |
| 5x5x5 | 8 vs 9 (generic: d = 6) | 5 | all (g = 2: complete) | not separated |
| 5x5x5 | 9 vs 10 (d13 agent) | 5, 6, 7, 8 (all g >= 2 components, all directions; d = 8: 1090) | line, flag, pflag, gline | not separated |
| 6x6x6 | 10..13 (generic, scan 2026-10-13: rank 10 lowest d = 8) | 5, 6, 7 (all g >= 2 components, all directions); d = 8 running for ranks 11..13 (rank 10 is separated generically at d = 8) | line, flag, pflag, gline (204 of 406 d = 7 Grassmannian pencils skipped by the size cap) | not separated |
| 7x7x7 | 12..18 | 5, 6 | line, flag, pflag, gline | not separated |
| 8x8x8 | 12..23 | 5, 6 (all g >= 2 components, all directions; ((3,2,1),(3,2,1),(2,1,1,1,1)): word minors allow ranks <= 16 only, checked for 12..15) | line, flag, pflag, gline (28 of 30 d = 6 Grassmannian pencils skipped by the size cap) | not separated |
| 9x9x9 | 13..29 | 5 | line, flag, pflag | not separated |
| 10x10x10 | 13..35 | 5 | line, flag, pflag | not separated |

Degrees "below the lowest separating degree" with g >= 2: 4x4x4 rank 6 (d = 5, 6, 7), 5x5x5 rank 8 (d = 5) and,
since the scan found 6x6x6 rank 10 vs 11 first at d = 8 (agents/to-all-Vstacks.md), 6x6x6 rank 10 (d = 5, 6, 7):
special U separate none of them, so these lowest separating degrees of the scan stand for special U as well.  (For
6x6x6..10x10x10 the ranks n+1.. are separated at d = 3, 4, 5, below which every Kronecker coefficient is <= 1.)

**Generic H- and V-stacks (U = generic subspace of every dim k = 1..g, including U = M).**  Every direction above
has the generic-U profile (H_k and V_k ranks for all k at the target ranks; special/res/*.jsonl, key 'generic'):
none separates (0 of 2208 directions), e.g. 5x5x5 rank 9 vs 10 at d = 6, 7, 8 (53 + 307 + 1090
directions, all of them; 1315 saturated: every H_k and V_k already at its bound min(n1, k n23), min(k n1, n23) at
rank 9), where the other sweeps ran without V-stacks (agents/to-all-Vstacks.md).

**A false alarm (2026-10-18), and a fix.**  5x5x5 d = 8 ((5,2,1),(4,2,1,1),(4,2,1,1)) dir 1 showed a generic
V-stack (U = M) of 17280 on a rank-9 vs 18205 on a rank-10 tensor -- it would have been a first separation of
rank 9 vs 10 -- but fresh tensors give 18205 at rank 9 as well (seeds 101 and 202, p = 524287 and 524269).  The
rank-9 tensor had been evaluated by two processes that chose different fillings (rankscan.Direction's choice
depends on PYTHONHASHSEED); a duplicated filling matrix capped the stack at 12 x 1440.  Now every process uses
PYTHONHASHSEED = 0 and checkpoints are keyed by the fillings.  The 9 earlier directions that may have mixed two
processes were rerun from scratch (2026-10-18/19; old records in special/superseded.jsonl): same outcome, not
separated.  The clean run of the component itself gives 18205 at rank 9 and 10.

**What the special loci look like.**
* Tensor-independent rank-drop loci of one functional exist in many components (4x4x4 d = 7: 22 of 210 directions
  on random lines, isolated codim-2 points in 12 of 87 g >= 3 directions; 5x5x5 rank 9: a few directions at d = 6,
  7, 83 of 958 at d = 8; 6x6x6 d = 7: 4 of 310 directions), but at every one of them a rank-r and a rank-(r+1)
  tensor lose the same rank (6x6x6 d = 7: the same rank at r = 10 and r = 14, e.g. 534, 750, 1134, 1680).  At the
  frontier for d <= 6 (6x6x6 and up) there is no tensor-independent drop point at all at the lowest frontier rank:
  every functional (and every natural U) is injective there, as the generic one.
* The codim-1 drop loci found are unions of hyperplanes of P(M^*) (all drop points rational on every line, g + 2
  lines span hyperplanes), including in the positive controls: C^4 d = 8 has two drop hyperplanes (220 and 84), the
  220-hyperplane U (dim 3) itself separates (H 285 vs 290, V 220 vs 225), and inside the 84-hyperplane there are
  planes P with "P + generic vector" separating (H3 299 vs 304, 274 vs 279).  C^3 d = 6: two drop lines.
* The plane method (resultants on a pencil of lines) also finds codim-2 loci that lines cannot: e.g. C^3 d = 6
  ((4,1,1),(3,2,1),(3,2,1)) g = 4 has a codim-2 locus with rank 9 on rank-4 vs 10 on rank-5 tensors (generic 10/10;
  rank 4 vs 5 is already separated generically at d = 5, so this is not new for the scan).
* specialU.py's swap eigenspaces were vacuous (its tau was the identity); here tau acts by exchanging the fillings,
  checked against the S^2 / Lambda^2 multiplicities from the character table.

**Not covered:** codim >= 2 loci for g >= 4 beyond random planes (codim 2) and lines through natural points;
Grassmannian pencils of the largest stacks (size cap: 204 of 406 in 6x6x6 d = 7, 18 of 30 in 7x7x7 d = 6, 28 of
30 in 8x8x8 d = 6); 8x8x8 d = 6 ((3,2,1),(3,2,1),(2,1,1,1,1)) at ranks 16..23 (beyond its word minors);
6x6x6 d >= 8, 7x7x7 d >= 7, 8x8x8 d >= 7, 9x9x9, 10x10x10 d >= 6.
